{"baseRefName":"dev","body":"## What this is\n\nFS2 research is now aligned with a simpler mental model: start from the forecasting question, run internet-scale retrieval for useful articles, and in parallel pull historic or proxy-historic data when the question classification supports it. The previous default path leaned on arXiv and GDELT and used heuristic domain routing, which made retrieval slower, noisier, and harder to reason about when integrations failed.\n\nThis change makes the default path faster and more predictable by using SearchAPI-backed web research and by only switching to a specialized domain pack when the question explicitly sets one. Time-bracket historic evidence no longer depends on GDELT; it uses dated news hits as a proxy event series when available.\n\nThe user-visible outcome is more reliable default evidence gathering for questions without custom domain metadata, and clearer control when teams do want a domain-specific pack.\n\n## Technical detail\n\n### Summary\n\n- Added `generic_web` default pack (SearchAPI news/scholar/books + optional web search); `generic_arxiv` manifest now points at the same implementation so legacy names still resolve to web retrieval.\n- Simplified `ResearchDomainRoutingAgent` to explicit `question.data.domain` only (no heuristic `routing_hint_patterns` or domain-profile routing); default pack is `generic_web`.\n- Removed GDELT from default multi-source packs (e.g. geopolitics, elections, macro trade balance) and replaced time-bracket `fetch_event_history` primary provider with SearchAPI news proxy events.\n- Updated architecture docs (`docs/architecture/fs2-research-agent.md`, `docs/architecture/02-pipeline.md`) and tests.\n\n### Process flow\n\n```mermaid\nflowchart TD\n  step2[STEP_2 research collect sources]\n  domain{question.data.domain explicit?}\n  specialized[Resolve named domain pack]\n  generic[Default generic_web pack SearchAPI]\n  retrieve[News scholar books optional web search]\n  historic{time_bracket question?}\n  proxy[SearchAPI news dated proxy events]\n  done[Persist sources for downstream FS3]\n  step2 --> domain\n  domain -->|yes| specialized --> retrieve\n  domain -->|no| generic --> retrieve\n  retrieve --> historic\n  historic -->|yes| proxy --> done\n  historic -->|no| done\n```\n\n### Approach\n\n- `retrieval_packs.py`: `_TemplateFallbackPack` and `GenericWebRetrievalPack` use `_GENERIC_WEB_SPEC`; geopolitics/elections multi specs drop GDELT.\n- `domain_router.py`: single explicit-metadata resolver; unknown explicit domain still raises.\n- `historical_data.py`: `_try_searchapi_news_events` builds deduped dated events from `get_searchapi_news_candidates`.\n- Defaults for `fallback_pack_name` / manifest fallbacks updated from `generic_arxiv` to `generic_web` where applicable.\n\n### Tests\n\n- FS2-focused pytest subset (routing, historical data, analyst review, maintainability budget, regime smoke, stage outcomes, FS2 replay): green before commit.\n- Full `pytest --ignore=tests/frontend` run was started in agent environment; complete locally or rely on CI before marking ready.\n\n### Verification\n\n- [x] `uv run ruff check .`\n- [x] `uv run ruff format --check .`\n- [ ] `uv run python -m pytest --ignore=tests/frontend -x -q` (full suite — run locally or confirm CI green before ready-for-review)\n\n### Risks\n\n- Questions without `question.data.domain` always use `generic_web`; teams that relied on heuristic routing must set domain explicitly for specialized packs.\n- SearchAPI news proxy for `time_bracket` is a weaker signal than GDELT for some topics; KM priors may fall back to ontology more often when news dates are sparse.\n\n### Branch-merge\n\n- Targets `dev`. No merge performed.\n\n### Out of scope (deliberately)\n\n- Task 1 (probability test literals) and Task 3 (question completeness validator) — not included in this PR.\n- GDELT client module and `GdeltRetrievalPack` class remain for potential non-default use; not wired into default STEP_2 paths.\n\n@kungfusaini please review.\n\nMade with [Cursor](https://cursor.com)\n","headRefName":"cross-fs/fs2-web-research-redesign","mergeStateStatus":"DIRTY","mergeable":"CONFLICTING","title":"FS2: Simplify FS2 web research retrieval","url":"https://github.com/elijahintelligence/elijah/pull/290"}

68	def _norm(text: str) -> str:
69	    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", " ", str(text or "").lower()).strip()
70	
71	
72	def _strip_driver_prefix(label: str) -> str:
73	    return re.sub(r"^\s*driver\s*:\s*", "", str(label or "").strip(), flags=re.IGNORECASE)
74	
75	
76	def _driver_nodes_index(graph: Any, *, question_id: str) -> tuple[dict[str, NodeId], dict[str, NodeId], dict[str, NodeId]]:
77	    by_title: dict[str, NodeId] = {}
78	    by_query: dict[str, NodeId] = {}
79	    by_id: dict[str, NodeId] = {}
80	    for node in getattr(graph, "nodes", {}).values():
81	        if str(getattr(node, "type", "")).strip() != "driver":
82	            continue
83	        data = getattr(node, "data", {}) or {}
84	        if node_question_id(data) != question_id:
85	            continue
86	        node_id = str(getattr(node, "id", "")).strip()
87	        if node_id:
88	            by_id[node_id] = node_id
89	        label = str(getattr(node, "label", "") or "").strip()
90	        for candidate in (
91	            label,
92	            _strip_driver_prefix(label),
93	            str(node_detail_value(data, "summary") or "").strip(),
94	            str(node_detail_value(data, "event") or "").strip(),
95	        ):
96	            key = _norm(candidate)
97	            if key and key not in by_title:
98	                by_title[key] = node_id
99	        search_query = _norm(str(node_detail_value(data, "search_query") or "").strip())
100	        if search_query and search_query not in by_query:
101	            by_query[search_query] = node_id
102	    return by_title, by_query, by_id
103	
104	
105	def _indicator_identity_key(*, driver_id: str, indicator_label: str) -> str:
106	    return f"{str(driver_id or '').strip()}\x1f{_norm(indicator_label)}"
107	
108	
109	def _legacy_indicator_identity_key(indicator_label: str) -> str:
110	    return f"legacy\x1f{_norm(indicator_label)}"
111	
112	
113	def _existing_indicator_nodes(graph: Any, *, question_id: str) -> dict[str, NodeId]:
114	    out: dict[str, NodeId] = {}
115	    for node in getattr(graph, "nodes", {}).values():
116	        if str(getattr(node, "type", "")).strip() != "indicator":
117	            continue
118	        data = getattr(node, "data", {}) or {}
119	        if node_question_id(data) != question_id:
120	            continue
121	        detail = dict(data.get("detail") or {}) if isinstance(data.get("detail"), dict) else {}
122	        driver_id = str(detail.get("parent_driver_id") or detail.get("driver_id") or "").strip()
123	        label = str(getattr(node, "label", "") or "").strip()
124	        key = _indicator_identity_key(driver_id=driver_id, indicator_label=label) if driver_id else _legacy_indicator_identity_key(label)
125	        if key and key not in out:
126	            out[key] = str(getattr(node, "id", "")).strip()
127	    return out
128	
129	
130	def _normalize_selected_sources(items: object) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
131	    out: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
132	    seen: set[tuple[str, ...]] = set()
133	    for item in items or []:
134	        if not isinstance(item, dict):
135	            continue
136	        normalized = {str(key): value for key, value in item.items() if value not in (None, "", [], {})}
137	        if not normalized:
138	            continue
139	        key = (
140	            str(normalized.get("title") or "").strip(),
141	            str(normalized.get("entry_id") or "").strip(),
142	            str(normalized.get("pdf_url") or "").strip(),
143	            str(normalized.get("pdf_filename") or normalized.get("local_path") or "").strip(),
144	        )
145	        if key in seen:
146	            continue
147	        seen.add(key)
148	        out.append(normalized)
149	    return out
150	
151	
152	def _merged_sources(existing: object, incoming: object) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
153	    merged = _normalize_selected_sources(existing)
154	    existing_keys = {
155	        (
156	            str(item.get("title") or "").strip(),
157	            str(item.get("entry_id") or "").strip(),
158	            str(item.get("pdf_url") or "").strip(),
159	            str(item.get("pdf_filename") or item.get("local_path") or "").strip(),
160	        )
161	        for item in merged
162	    }
163	    for item in _normalize_selected_sources(incoming):
164	        key = (
165	            str(item.get("title") or "").strip(),
166	            str(item.get("entry_id") or "").strip(),
167	            str(item.get("pdf_url") or "").strip(),
168	            str(item.get("pdf_filename") or item.get("local_path") or "").strip(),
169	        )
170	        if key in existing_keys:
171	            continue
172	        existing_keys.add(key)
173	        merged.append(item)
174	    return merged
175	
176	
177	def _edge_key(edge_type: str, from_id: str, to_id: str) -> tuple[str, str, str]:
178	    return (edge_type, from_id, to_id)
179	
180	
181	def _source_key(value: object) -> str:
182	    return str(value or "").strip().lower()
183	
184	
185	def _source_keys_from_payload(source: Mapping[str, Any], *, label: str = "", detail: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None) -> set[str]:
186	    detail_payload = dict(detail or {})
187	    keys: set[str] = set()
188	    candidates = [
189	        label,
190	        source.get("source_id"),
191	        source.get("evidence_node_id"),
192	        source.get("document_key"),
193	        source.get("title"),
194	        source.get("entry_id"),
195	        source.get("pdf_url"),
196	        source.get("source_url"),
197	        source.get("pdf_filename"),
198	        source.get("filename"),
199	        source.get("local_path"),
200	        source.get("source_path"),
201	        detail_payload.get("document_key"),
202	        detail_payload.get("source_id"),
203	        detail_payload.get("evidence_node_id"),
204	        detail_payload.get("document_link"),
205	        detail_payload.get("filename"),
206	        detail_payload.get("title"),
207	        detail_payload.get("source_url"),
208	        detail_payload.get("pdf_url"),
209	    ]
210	    try:
211	        candidates.append(canonical_document_key_from_source(source=source, detail=detail_payload, label=label))
212	    except Exception:
213	        pass
214	    for candidate in candidates:
215	        text = str(candidate or "").strip()
216	        if not text:
217	            continue
218	        keys.add(_source_key(text))
219	        normalized = _norm(text)
220	        if normalized:
221	            keys.add(normalized)
222	        basename = Path(text).name
223	        if basename and basename != text:
224	            keys.add(_source_key(basename))
225	            normalized_basename = _norm(basename)
226	            if normalized_basename:
227	                keys.add(normalized_basename)
228	    return keys
229	
230	
231	def _information_source_lookup(graph: Any, *, question_id: str) -> dict[str, str]:
232	    lookup: dict[str, str] = {}
233	    for node in getattr(graph, "nodes", {}).values():
234	        if str(getattr(node, "type", "") or "").strip().lower() not in {"evidence", "information"}:
235	            continue
236	        data = getattr(node, "data", {}) or {}
237	        if node_question_id(data) != question_id:
238	            continue
239	        node_id = str(getattr(node, "id", "") or "").strip()
240	        if not node_id:
241	            continue
242	        label = str(getattr(node, "label", "") or "").strip()
243	        detail = dict(data.get("detail") or {}) if isinstance(data.get("detail"), dict) else {}
244	        provenance = node_provenance(data)
245	        source_items = [item for item in (provenance.get("sources") or []) if isinstance(item, Mapping)] or [{}]
246	        node_keys = {node_id, _source_key(node_id), _norm(node_id)}
247	        try:
248	            node_keys.add(document_key_from_node(label=label, data=data))
249	        except Exception:
250	            pass
251	        for source in source_items:
252	            node_keys.update(_source_keys_from_payload(source, label=label, detail=detail))
253	        for key in node_keys:
254	            if key and key not in lookup:
255	                lookup[key] = node_id
256	    return lookup
257	
258	
259	def _resolved_information_ids_for_sources(
260	    *,
261	    selected_sources: list[dict[str, Any]],
262	    information_lookup: dict[str, str],
263	) -> list[str]:
264	    resolved: list[str] = []
265	    seen: set[str] = set()
266	    for source in selected_sources:
267	        for key in _source_keys_from_payload(source, label=str(source.get("title") or "")):
268	            node_id = information_lookup.get(key)
269	            if not node_id or node_id in seen:
270	                continue
271	            seen.add(node_id)
272	            resolved.append(node_id)
273	            break
274	    return resolved
275	
276	
277	def _indicator_is_inferred(indicator_row: Mapping[str, Any]) -> bool:
278	    source_mode = str(indicator_row.get("source_mode") or "").strip()
279	    epistemic_status = str(indicator_row.get("epistemic_status") or "").strip()
280	    return source_mode in {"pack_fallback_indicator", "llm_best_effort_no_sources"} or epistemic_status == "hypothesis"
281	
282	
283	def _create_indicator_reference_edges(
284	    graph: Any,
285	    *,
286	    ind: Mapping[str, Any],
287	    indicator_id: str,
288	    selected_sources: list[dict[str, Any]],
289	    information_lookup: dict[str, str],
290	    toi: datetime | None,
291	    cfg: IndicatorGraphApplicationConfig,
292	) -> tuple[int, int]:
293	    if _indicator_is_inferred(ind):
294	        return 0, 0
295	    information_ids = _resolved_information_ids_for_sources(selected_sources=selected_sources, information_lookup=information_lookup)
296	    created = 0
297	    skipped = 0
298	    for information_id in information_ids:
299	        if cfg.dedupe_edges and getattr(graph, "_edge_keys", None) is not None:
300	            if _edge_key(cfg.reference_edge_type, information_id, indicator_id) in graph._edge_keys:
301	                skipped += 1
302	                continue
303	        graph.new_edge(
304	            from_id=information_id,
305	            to_id=indicator_id,
306	            edge_type=cfg.reference_edge_type,
307	            weight=1.0,
308	            toi=toi,
309	            reference_type="source",
310	        )
311	        created += 1
312	    return created, skipped
313	
314	
315	def _strip_indicator_relation_detail(data: object) -> dict[str, Any]:
316	    payload = dict(data or {}) if isinstance(data, dict) else {}
317	    payload.pop("rationale", None)
318	    payload.pop("confidence", None)
319	    detail = dict(payload.get("detail") or {}) if isinstance(payload.get("detail"), dict) else {}
320	    detail.pop("rationale", None)
321	    detail.pop("confidence", None)
322	    if detail:
323	        payload["detail"] = detail
324	    else:
325	        payload.pop("detail", None)
326	    return payload
327	
328	
329	def _indicator_detail(
330	    indicator_row: Mapping[str, Any],
331	    *,
332	    parent_driver_id: str = "",
333	    parent_driver_label: str = "",
334	) -> dict[str, Any]:
335	    detail: dict[str, Any] = {}
336	    for key in ("source_mode", "epistemic_status", "human_review_rationale", "movement_direction"):
337	        value = str(indicator_row.get(key) or "").strip()
338	        if value:
339	            detail[key] = value
340	    if str(parent_driver_id or "").strip():
341	        detail["indicator_scope"] = "driver"
342	        detail["parent_driver_id"] = str(parent_driver_id or "").strip()
343	    if str(parent_driver_label or "").strip():
344	        detail["parent_driver_label"] = str(parent_driver_label or "").strip()
345	    return detail
346

348	@dataclass
349	class _IndicatorApplyStats:
350	    """Mutable accumulator for indicator application counts."""
351	
352	    indicators_added: int = 0
353	    edges_added: int = 0
354	    driver_rows_processed: int = 0
355	    missing_driver_rows: int = 0
356	    fallback_indicator_count: int = 0
357	    llm_indicator_count: int = 0
358	    review_required_driver_rows: int = 0
359	    fallback_driver_rows: int = 0
360	    drivers_without_indicators: int = 0
361	    quality_blocked_driver_rows: int = 0
362	    budget_skipped_driver_rows: int = 0
363	    generic_indicator_rejections: int = 0
364	    duplicate_indicator_rejections: int = 0
365	    positive_indicator_candidate_count: int = 0
366	    negative_indicator_candidate_count: int = 0
367	    indicator_reference_edges_added: int = 0
368	    indicator_reference_edges_skipped: int = 0
369	    quality_flags: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
370	
371	
372	def _retrieval_placeholder_reason(row: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
373	    status = str(row.get("status") or "").strip()
374	    placeholder_reason = str(row.get("placeholder_reason") or "").strip()
375	    if status in _RETRIEVAL_PLACEHOLDER_STATUSES:
376	        return status
377	    if placeholder_reason in _RETRIEVAL_PLACEHOLDER_STATUSES:
378	        return placeholder_reason
379	    if row.get("retrieval_attempted") is False and (status or placeholder_reason):
380	        return placeholder_reason or status or "indicator_retrieval_skipped_budget"
381	    return ""
382	
383	
384	def _placeholder_quality_flag(reason: str) -> str:
385	    if reason == "indicator_retrieval_disabled":
386	        return "source_retrieval_disabled"
387	    if reason == "indicator_retrieval_budget_zero":
388	        return "source_retrieval_budget_zero"
389	    return "source_retrieval_skipped_budget"
390	
391	
392	def _collect_eligible_drivers(
393	    rows: list[Any],
394	    *,
395	    driver_lookup: dict[str, str],
396	    driver_query_lookup: dict[str, str],
397	    driver_id_lookup: dict[str, str],
398	    per_driver: list[dict[str, Any]],
399	    stats: _IndicatorApplyStats,
400	) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], list[dict[str, Any]]]:
401	    """Match driver rows to graph nodes. Returns (eligible_drivers, eligible_meta)."""
402	    eligible_drivers: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
403	    eligible_meta: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
404	    for row in rows:
405	        if not isinstance(row, dict):
406	            continue
407	        driver_title = str(row.get("driver_title") or "").strip()
408	        driver_search_query = str(row.get("driver_search_query") or "").strip()
409	        if not driver_title and not driver_search_query:
410	            continue
411	        row_driver_id = str(row.get("driver_id") or row.get("graph_driver_id") or "").strip()
412	        driver_id = driver_id_lookup.get(row_driver_id, "") if row_driver_id else ""
413	        if not driver_id:
414	            driver_id = driver_lookup.get(_norm(driver_title), "")
415	        if not driver_id:
416	            query_key = _norm(driver_search_query)
417	            if query_key:
418	                driver_id = driver_query_lookup.get(query_key, "")
419	        if not driver_id:
420	            stats.missing_driver_rows += 1
421	            per_driver.append(
422	                {
423	                    "driver_title": driver_title,
424	                    "status": "missing_driver_node",
425	                    "indicator_count": 0,
426	                    "edges_added": 0,
427	                    "source_row_status": str(row.get("status") or "").strip(),
428	                }
429	            )
430	            continue
431	        selected_sources = _normalize_selected_sources(row.get("items") or row.get("selected_sources") or [])
432	        source_titles = [str(s.get("title") or "").strip() for s in selected_sources if str(s.get("title") or "").strip()]
433	        placeholder_reason = _retrieval_placeholder_reason(row)
434	        if placeholder_reason:
435	            quality_flag = _placeholder_quality_flag(placeholder_reason)
436	            stats.driver_rows_processed += 1
437	            stats.review_required_driver_rows += 1
438	            stats.drivers_without_indicators += 1
439	            if quality_flag == "source_retrieval_skipped_budget":
440	                stats.budget_skipped_driver_rows += 1
441	            stats.quality_flags.add(quality_flag)
442	            per_driver.append(
443	                {
444	                    "driver_title": driver_title,
445	                    "status": quality_flag,
446	                    "indicator_count": 0,
447	                    "indicators_created": 0,
448	                    "edges_added": 0,
449	                    "reference_edges_added": 0,
450	                    "fallback_used": False,
451	                    "review_required": True,
452	                    "message": "Indicator retrieval did not run for this driver; no best-effort indicators were applied.",
453	                    "source_row_status": str(row.get("status") or "").strip(),
454	                    "placeholder_reason": placeholder_reason,
455	                    "quality_flags": [quality_flag],
456	                    "stage_outcome": build_stage_outcome(
457	                        status="partial",
458	                        reason_code=quality_flag,
459	                        message="Indicator retrieval did not run for this driver; no best-effort indicators were applied.",
460	                        fallback_used=False,
461	                        quality_flags=[quality_flag],
462	                        extra={"placeholder_reason": placeholder_reason},
463	                    ),
464	                    "indicators": [],
465	                }
466	            )
467	            continue
468	        eligible_drivers.append({"driver_title": driver_title, "driver_query": driver_search_query, "source_titles": source_titles})
469	        eligible_meta.append({"row": row, "driver_id": driver_id, "driver_title": driver_title, "selected_sources": selected_sources})
470	    return eligible_drivers, eligible_meta
471	
472	
473	def _upsert_indicator_node(
474	    graph: Any,
475	    *,
476	    ind: Mapping[str, Any],
477	    indicator_lookup: dict[str, str],
478	    question_id: str,
479	    question_source: str,
480	    driver_id: str,
481	    driver_title: str,
482	    selected_sources: list[dict[str, Any]],
483	    toi: datetime | None,
484	) -> tuple[str, bool]:
485	    """Create or update an indicator node. Returns (indicator_id, was_created)."""
486	    name = str(ind.get("name") or "").strip()
487	    key = _indicator_identity_key(driver_id=driver_id, indicator_label=name)
488	    indicator_id = indicator_lookup.get(key, "")
489	    if not indicator_id:
490	        legacy_key = _legacy_indicator_identity_key(name)
491	        indicator_id = indicator_lookup.pop(legacy_key, "")
492	        if indicator_id:
493	            indicator_lookup[key] = indicator_id
494	    if not indicator_id:
495	        origin_stage = str(ind.get("origin_stage") or "indicator_extraction").strip() or "indicator_extraction"
496	        indicator_id = graph.new_node(
497	            "indicator",
498	            label=name,
499	            toi=toi,
500	            **build_node_data(
501	                question_id=question_id,
502	                question_source=question_source,
503	                origin=build_origin(producer="indicator_literature", stage=origin_stage, question_source=question_source),
504	                provenance=build_provenance(source_kind="driver_indicator_literature", sources=selected_sources),
505	                detail=_indicator_detail(ind, parent_driver_id=driver_id, parent_driver_label=driver_title),
506	            ),
507	        )
508	        indicator_lookup[key] = indicator_id
509	        return indicator_id, True
510	
511	    node = getattr(graph, "nodes", {}).get(indicator_id)
512	    if node is not None:
513	        existing_data = _strip_indicator_relation_detail(getattr(node, "data", {}) or {})
514	        merged_sources = _merged_sources(node_provenance(existing_data).get("sources"), selected_sources)
515	        merged_detail = dict(existing_data.get("detail") or {}) if isinstance(existing_data.get("detail"), dict) else {}
516	        merged_detail.update(_indicator_detail(ind, parent_driver_id=driver_id, parent_driver_label=driver_title))
517	        origin_stage = str(ind.get("origin_stage") or "indicator_extraction").strip() or "indicator_extraction"
518	        node.data = build_node_data(
519	            question_id=question_id,
520	            question_source=question_source,
521	            origin=build_origin(producer="indicator_literature", stage=origin_stage, question_source=question_source),
522	            provenance=build_provenance(source_kind="driver_indicator_literature", sources=merged_sources),
523	            detail=merged_detail or None,
524	            extra=existing_data,
525	        )
526	    return indicator_id, False
527	
528	
529	def _create_indicator_edge(
530	    graph: Any,
531	    *,
532	    ind: Mapping[str, Any],
533	    indicator_id: str,
534	    driver_id: str,
535	    selected_sources: list[dict[str, Any]],
536	    toi: datetime | None,
537	    cfg: IndicatorGraphApplicationConfig,
538	) -> None:
539	    """Create a CONDITIONAL edge from indicator to driver."""
540	    direction = str(ind.get("direction") or "supports").strip().lower()
541	    if direction not in {"supports", "contradicts"}:
542	        direction = "supports"
543	    rj = normalize_relationship_judgment(ind.get("relationship_judgment"), fallback=relationship_judgment_from_direction(direction))
544	    odds = relationship_judgment_odds_multiplier(rj, fallback=relationship_judgment_from_direction(direction))
545	    extraction_confidence = ind.get("confidence")
546	    edge_data: dict[str, Any] = {
547	        "relation": "indicator_to_driver",
548	        "direction": direction,
549	        "movement_direction": str(ind.get("movement_direction") or "positive").strip().lower() or "positive",
550	        "relationship_judgment": rj,
551	        "odds_multiplier": odds,
552	        "relationship_description": str(ind.get("relationship_description") or "").strip() or str(ind.get("rationale") or "").strip(),
553	        "rationale": str(ind.get("rationale") or "").strip(),
554	        "confidence": extraction_confidence,
555	        "extraction_confidence": extraction_confidence,
556	        "sources": selected_sources,
557	    }
558	    edge_data["edge_quality"] = pending_conditional_edge_quality(reason="deferred_until_fs4_baselines")
559	    graph.new_edge(
560	        from_id=indicator_id,
561	        to_id=driver_id,
562	        edge_type=cfg.conditional_edge_type,
563	        weight=float(cfg.conditional_edge_weight) * odds,
564	        toi=toi,
565	        **edge_data,
566	    )
567	
568	
569	def _build_driver_summary(
570	    *,
571	    driver_title: str,
572	    indicators: list[dict[str, Any]],
573	    created_for_driver: int,
574	    edges_for_driver: int,
575	    reference_edges_added: int,
576	    extraction_payload: dict[str, Any],
577	    source_row_status: str,
578	) -> dict[str, Any]:
579	    """Build the per-driver summary dict for the output payload."""
580	    return {
581	        "driver_title": driver_title,
582	        "status": "ok" if indicators else "review_required_no_fallback",
583	        "indicator_count": len([x for x in indicators if isinstance(x, dict)]),
584	        "indicators_created": created_for_driver,
585	        "edges_added": edges_for_driver,
586	        "reference_edges_added": reference_edges_added,
587	        "fallback_used": bool(extraction_payload.get("fallback_used")),
588	        "review_required": bool(extraction_payload.get("review_required")),
589	        "message": str(extraction_payload.get("message") or "").strip(),
590	        "pack_name": str(extraction_payload.get("pack_name") or "").strip(),
591	        "fallback_policy": str(extraction_payload.get("fallback_policy") or "").strip(),
592	        "fallback_support": str(extraction_payload.get("fallback_support") or "").strip(),
593	        "stage_outcome": dict(extraction_payload.get("stage_outcome") or {}),
594	        "quality_gate": dict(extraction_payload.get("quality_gate") or {}),
595	        "quality_flags": list(dict.fromkeys(dict(extraction_payload.get("stage_outcome") or {}).get("quality_flags") or [])),
596	        "source_row_status": source_row_status,
597	        "indicators": [
598	            {
599	                "name": str(i.get("name") or "").strip(),
600	                "direction": str(i.get("direction") or "supports").strip(),
601	                "movement_direction": str(i.get("movement_direction") or "positive").strip(),
602	                "relationship_judgment": str(i.get("relationship_judgment") or "").strip(),
603	                "rationale": str(i.get("rationale") or "").strip(),
604	                "confidence": i.get("confidence"),
605	                "source_mode": str(i.get("source_mode") or "").strip(),
606	                "epistemic_status": str(i.get("epistemic_status") or "").strip(),
607	            }
608	            for i in indicators
609	            if isinstance(i, dict) and str(i.get("name") or "").strip()
610	        ],
611	    }
612	
613	
614	def _apply_single_driver_extraction(
615	    graph: Any,
616	    *,
617	    meta: dict[str, Any],
618	    extraction_payload: dict[str, Any],
619	    indicator_lookup: dict[str, str],
620	    question_id: str,
621	    question_source: str,
622	    toi: datetime | None,
623	    cfg: IndicatorGraphApplicationConfig,
624	    stats: _IndicatorApplyStats,
625	    information_lookup: dict[str, str],
626	) -> dict[str, Any]:
627	    driver_id, driver_title, selected_sources = meta["driver_id"], meta["driver_title"], meta["selected_sources"]
628	    raw_indicators = [x for x in (extraction_payload.get("indicators") or []) if isinstance(x, dict)]
629	    review_counted = bool(extraction_payload.get("review_required"))
630	    if review_counted:
631	        stats.review_required_driver_rows += 1
632	    if extraction_payload.get("fallback_used"):
633	        stats.fallback_driver_rows += 1
634	    quality = apply_indicator_quality_gate(rows=raw_indicators, driver_title=driver_title)
635	    stats.generic_indicator_rejections += int(quality.get("generic_indicator_rejections") or 0)
636	    stats.duplicate_indicator_rejections += int(quality.get("duplicate_indicator_rejections") or 0)
637	    stats.positive_indicator_candidate_count += int(quality.get("positive_indicator_count") or 0)
638	    stats.negative_indicator_candidate_count += int(quality.get("negative_indicator_count") or 0)
639	    stats.quality_flags.update(str(flag) for flag in (quality.get("quality_flags") or []) if str(flag))
640	    if not bool(quality.get("passed")):
641	        if not review_counted:
642	            stats.review_required_driver_rows += 1
643	        stats.quality_blocked_driver_rows += 1
644	        stats.driver_rows_processed += 1
645	        stats.drivers_without_indicators += 1
646	        blocked_payload = dict(extraction_payload)
647	        blocked_payload["indicators"] = []
648	        blocked_payload["review_required"] = True
649	        blocked_payload["quality_gate"] = dict(quality)
650	        blocked_payload["stage_outcome"] = build_stage_outcome(
651	            status="partial",
652	            reason_code="indicator_quality_gate_failed",
653	            message="Indicator candidates did not satisfy the minimum count and movement balance gate.",
654	            fallback_used=bool(extraction_payload.get("fallback_used")),
655	            quality_flags=list(dict.fromkeys(quality.get("quality_flags") or ["indicator_quality_gate_failed"])),
656	            extra={"quality_gate": dict(quality)},
657	        )
658	        return _build_driver_summary(
659	            driver_title=driver_title,
660	            indicators=[],
661	            created_for_driver=0,
662	            edges_for_driver=0,
663	            extraction_payload=blocked_payload,
664	            source_row_status=str(meta["row"].get("status") or "").strip(),
665	            reference_edges_added=0,
666	        )
667	    indicators = balanced_indicator_subset(
668	        list(quality.get("indicators") or []),
669	        max_indicators=effective_indicator_limit(cfg.max_indicators_per_driver),
670	    )
671	    extraction_payload = {**dict(extraction_payload), "indicators": indicators, "quality_gate": dict(quality)}
672	    created_for_driver = 0
673	    edges_for_driver = 0
674	    reference_edges_for_driver = 0
675	    for ind in indicators:
676	        if not isinstance(ind, dict):
677	            continue
678	        name = str(ind.get("name") or "").strip()
679	        if not name or not _norm(name):
680	            continue
681	        if str(ind.get("source_mode") or "").strip() == "pack_fallback_indicator":
682	            stats.fallback_indicator_count += 1
683	        else:
684	            stats.llm_indicator_count += 1
685	        indicator_id, was_created = _upsert_indicator_node(
686	            graph,
687	            ind=ind,
688	            indicator_lookup=indicator_lookup,
689	            question_id=question_id,
690	            question_source=question_source,
691	            driver_id=driver_id,
692	            driver_title=driver_title,
693	            selected_sources=selected_sources,
694	            toi=toi,
695	        )
696	        if was_created:
697	            stats.indicators_added += 1
698	            created_for_driver += 1
699	        reference_edges_added, reference_edges_skipped = _create_indicator_reference_edges(
700	            graph,
701	            ind=ind,
702	            indicator_id=indicator_id,
703	            selected_sources=selected_sources,
704	            information_lookup=information_lookup,
705	            toi=toi,
706	            cfg=cfg,
707	        )
708	        stats.indicator_reference_edges_added += reference_edges_added
709	        stats.indicator_reference_edges_skipped += reference_edges_skipped
710	        reference_edges_for_driver += reference_edges_added
711	        if cfg.dedupe_edges and getattr(graph, "_edge_keys", None) is not None:
712	            if _edge_key(cfg.conditional_edge_type, indicator_id, driver_id) in graph._edge_keys:
713	                continue
714	        _create_indicator_edge(
715	            graph, ind=ind, indicator_id=indicator_id, driver_id=driver_id, selected_sources=selected_sources, toi=toi, cfg=cfg
716	        )
717	        stats.edges_added += 1
718	        edges_for_driver += 1
719	
720	    stats.driver_rows_processed += 1
721	    if not indicators:
722	        stats.drivers_without_indicators += 1
723	    return _build_driver_summary(
724	        driver_title=driver_title,
725	        indicators=indicators,
726	        created_for_driver=created_for_driver,
727	        edges_for_driver=edges_for_driver,
728	        extraction_payload=extraction_payload,
729	        source_row_status=str(meta["row"].get("status") or "").strip(),
730	        reference_edges_added=reference_edges_for_driver,
731	    )

734	def _compute_indicator_stage_outcome(
735	    *,
736	    per_driver_stage_statuses: list[str],
737	    stats: _IndicatorApplyStats,
738	) -> dict[str, Any]:
739	    s = stats
740	    ce: dict[str, Any] = {
741	        "driver_rows_processed": s.driver_rows_processed,
742	        "llm_indicator_candidate_count": s.llm_indicator_count,
743	        "fallback_indicator_candidate_count": s.fallback_indicator_count,
744	        "quality_blocked_driver_rows": s.quality_blocked_driver_rows,
745	        "budget_skipped_driver_rows": s.budget_skipped_driver_rows,
746	        "generic_indicator_rejections": s.generic_indicator_rejections,
747	        "duplicate_indicator_rejections": s.duplicate_indicator_rejections,
748	        "positive_indicator_candidate_count": s.positive_indicator_candidate_count,
749	        "negative_indicator_candidate_count": s.negative_indicator_candidate_count,
750	    }
751	    quality_flags = list(dict.fromkeys(sorted(s.quality_flags)))
752	    if not per_driver_stage_statuses and s.driver_rows_processed == 0:
753	        return build_stage_outcome(
754	            status="failed",
755	            reason_code="indicator_no_driver_rows",
756	            message="Indicator extraction did not process any driver rows.",
757	            fallback_used=False,
758	            quality_flags=["no_driver_rows"],
759	            extra={
760	                "driver_rows_processed": s.driver_rows_processed,
761	                "missing_driver_rows": s.missing_driver_rows,
762	                "drivers_without_indicators": s.drivers_without_indicators,
763	            },
764	        )
765	    if s.quality_blocked_driver_rows > 0:
766	        return build_stage_outcome(
767	            status="partial",
768	            reason_code="indicator_quality_gate_failed",
769	            message="Indicator extraction blocked one or more driver rows that failed the minimum count or movement-balance gate.",
770	            fallback_used=s.fallback_driver_rows > 0,
771	            quality_flags=quality_flags or ["indicator_quality_gate_failed"],
772	            extra={
773	                **ce,
774	                "missing_driver_rows": s.missing_driver_rows,
775	                "drivers_without_indicators": s.drivers_without_indicators,
776	                "review_required_driver_rows": s.review_required_driver_rows,
777	            },
778	        )
779	    if s.budget_skipped_driver_rows > 0:
780	        return build_stage_outcome(
781	            status="partial",
782	            reason_code="indicator_source_retrieval_skipped_budget",
783	            message="Indicator retrieval budget skipped one or more driver rows; no best-effort indicators were applied for those rows.",
784	            fallback_used=s.fallback_driver_rows > 0,
785	            quality_flags=quality_flags or ["source_retrieval_skipped_budget"],
786	            extra={
787	                **ce,
788	                "missing_driver_rows": s.missing_driver_rows,
789	                "drivers_without_indicators": s.drivers_without_indicators,
790	                "review_required_driver_rows": s.review_required_driver_rows,
791	            },
792	        )
793	    if (
794	        s.review_required_driver_rows > 0
795	        and s.review_required_driver_rows == s.driver_rows_processed
796	        and s.drivers_without_indicators == s.driver_rows_processed
797	        and s.missing_driver_rows == 0
798	    ):
799	        return build_stage_outcome(
800	            status="partial",
801	            reason_code="indicator_review_required_no_candidates",
802	            message="Indicator extraction could not ground indicators for the processed drivers.",
803	            fallback_used=False,
804	            quality_flags=quality_flags or ["review_required_driver_rows", "no_indicator_candidates"],
805	            extra={
806	                **ce,
807	                "review_required_driver_rows": s.review_required_driver_rows,
808	                "drivers_without_indicators": s.drivers_without_indicators,
809	            },
810	        )
811	    if s.drivers_without_indicators > 0:
812	        return build_stage_outcome(
813	            status="failed",
814	            reason_code="indicator_missing_for_driver_rows",
815	            message="Indicator extraction did not produce at least one usable indicator for every driver row.",
816	            fallback_used=s.fallback_driver_rows > 0,
817	            quality_flags=(quality_flags or ["missing_indicator_driver_rows"])
818	            + (["pack_fallback_used"] if s.fallback_driver_rows > 0 else []),
819	            extra={
820	                **ce,
821	                "missing_driver_rows": s.missing_driver_rows,
822	                "drivers_without_indicators": s.drivers_without_indicators,
823	                "review_required_driver_rows": s.review_required_driver_rows,
824	            },
825	        )
826	    if s.missing_driver_rows > 0:
827	        return build_stage_outcome(
828	            status="partial",
829	            reason_code="indicator_missing_driver_nodes",
830	            message="Indicator extraction completed for matched driver rows, but some literature rows could not be matched to driver nodes.",
831	            fallback_used=s.fallback_driver_rows > 0,
832	            quality_flags=["missing_driver_nodes"] + quality_flags + (["pack_fallback_used"] if s.fallback_driver_rows > 0 else []),
833	            extra={
834	                **ce,
835	                "missing_driver_rows": s.missing_driver_rows,
836	                "drivers_without_indicators": s.drivers_without_indicators,
837	                "review_required_driver_rows": s.review_required_driver_rows,
838	            },
839	        )
840	    if all(st == "ok" for st in per_driver_stage_statuses):
841	        return build_stage_outcome(
842	            status="ok",
843	            reason_code="indicator_llm_generated",
844	            message="Indicator extraction completed without degraded paths.",
845	            fallback_used=False,
846	            quality_flags=[],
847	            extra={**ce, "drivers_without_indicators": s.drivers_without_indicators},
848	        )
849	    if s.review_required_driver_rows > 0:
850	        return build_stage_outcome(
851	            status="partial",
852	            reason_code="indicator_review_required_driver_rows",
853	            message="Indicator extraction completed with review-required driver rows.",
854	            fallback_used=s.fallback_driver_rows > 0,
855	            quality_flags=quality_flags or ["review_required_driver_rows"],
856	            extra={
857	                **ce,
858	                "review_required_driver_rows": s.review_required_driver_rows,
859	                "drivers_without_indicators": s.drivers_without_indicators,
860	            },
861	        )
862	    if any(st in ("ok", "partial") for st in per_driver_stage_statuses):
863	        return build_stage_outcome(
864	            status="partial",
865	            reason_code="indicator_degraded_driver_rows",
866	            message="Indicator extraction completed with degraded driver rows.",
867	            fallback_used=s.fallback_driver_rows > 0,
868	            quality_flags=quality_flags + (["pack_fallback_used"] if s.fallback_driver_rows > 0 else []),
869	            extra={
870	                **ce,
871	                "review_required_driver_rows": s.review_required_driver_rows,
872	                "drivers_without_indicators": s.drivers_without_indicators,
873	            },
874	        )
875	    return build_stage_outcome(
876	        status="failed",
877	        reason_code="indicator_no_candidates",
878	        message="Indicator extraction produced no usable indicators for the processed drivers.",
879	        fallback_used=False,
880	        quality_flags=["no_indicator_candidates"],
881	        extra={
882	            **ce,
883	            "review_required_driver_rows": s.review_required_driver_rows,
884	            "drivers_without_indicators": s.drivers_without_indicators,
885	        },
886	    )

1	"""Helpers for seeding the test-question + replay fixture set into the DB."""
2	
3	from __future__ import annotations
4	
5	import json
6	import os
7	import tomllib
8	from dataclasses import dataclass
9	from pathlib import Path
10	from typing import Any
11	
12	from packages.storage import (
13	    QuestionRecord,
14	    QuestionSetRecord,
15	    StorageRuntimeConfig,
16	    WorkerStorageAdapters,
17	    build_database_worker_storage_adapters,
18	)
19	
20	TEST_QUESTION_SET_ID = "test_questions"
21	TEST_QUESTION_SET_LABEL = "FS4 Test Questions"
22	TEST_QUESTION_SET_DESCRIPTION = "DB-seeded replay fixture questions for FS2/FS4 process tests."
23	
24	TEST_QUESTION_IDS: tuple[str, ...] = (
25	    "tq_cat_2024_us_election",
26	    "tq_bin_2023_us_recession",
27	    "tq_cat_fomc_dec_2024",
28	    "tq_time_covid_lockdown_us",
29	    "tq_time_post_ian_hurricane",
30	    "tq_time_post_covid_drawdown",
31	    "tq_num_cpi_yoy_eoy_2023",
32	    "tq_num_brent_eoy_2023",
33	    "tq_num_us10y_eoy_2023",
34	)
35	
36	_TEST_QUESTIONS_PATH = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "data" / "test_questions.toml"
37	_RECORDINGS_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "data" / "test_questions_fs2_recordings"
38	
39	
40	@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
41	class SeededTestQuestionFixture:
42	    storage_runtime: StorageRuntimeConfig
43	    adapters: WorkerStorageAdapters
44	    graphs_dir: Path
45	    questions_path: Path
46	
47	
48	def _load_seed_rows() -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
49	    with _TEST_QUESTIONS_PATH.open("rb") as handle:
50	        payload = tomllib.load(handle)
51	    rows = list(payload.get("questions") or [])
52	    return [dict(row) for row in rows if isinstance(row, dict)]
53	
54	
55	def _load_recording(question_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
56	    path = _RECORDINGS_DIR / f"{question_id}.json"
57	    with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
58	        payload = json.load(handle)
59	    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
60	        raise ValueError(f"Replay fixture for {question_id!r} must be a JSON object")
61	    return payload
62	
63	
64	def _data_mode_for(qtype: str) -> str:
65	    return "categorical_events" if qtype in {"binary", "categorical_outcome"} else "bucketed_series"
66	
67	
68	def _question_buckets(row: dict[str, Any]) -> list[Any] | None:
69	    if isinstance(row.get("time_buckets"), list):
70	        return list(row["time_buckets"])
71	    if isinstance(row.get("target_buckets"), list):
72	        return list(row["target_buckets"])
73	    return None
74	
75	
76	def _question_record_from_seed(*, row: dict[str, Any], seed_order: int) -> QuestionRecord:
77	    question_id = str(row.get("id") or "").strip()
78	    replay_payload = _load_recording(question_id)
79	    replay_features = replay_payload.get("question_features")
80	    if not isinstance(replay_features, dict):
81	        raise ValueError(f"Replay fixture for {question_id!r} must contain 'question_features'")
82	
83	    qtype = str(row.get("qtype") or "").strip()
84	    resolved_at = str(row.get("resolved_at") or "").strip()
85	    metadata = {
86	        "question_id": question_id,
87	        "question_set": TEST_QUESTION_SET_ID,
88	        "question_set_label": TEST_QUESTION_SET_LABEL,
89	        "domain": "general",
90	        "keywords": [],
91	        "correct_answer": str(row.get("correct_answer") or "").strip(),
92	        "resolved_at": resolved_at,
93	        "as_of": str(row.get("as_of") or "").strip(),
94	        "phase": str(row.get("phase") or "").strip(),
95	        "expected_fs2_fields": list(row.get("expected_fs2_fields") or []),
96	        "expected_fs4_estimator": str(row.get("expected_fs4_estimator") or "").strip(),
97	        "seed_order": int(seed_order),
98	        "fixture_source": "tests/data/test_questions.toml",
99	        "replay_source": f"tests/data/test_questions_fs2_recordings/{question_id}.json",
100	        "replay_question_features": dict(replay_features),
101	    }
102	    if isinstance(replay_payload.get("_meta"), dict):
103	        metadata["replay_meta"] = dict(replay_payload["_meta"])
104	
105	    return QuestionRecord(
106	        question_id=question_id,
107	        url=f"local://test-questions/{question_id}",
108	        label=str(row.get("title") or "").strip(),
109	        toi=f"{resolved_at}T00:00:00Z" if resolved_at else None,
110	        target_date=f"{resolved_at}T00:00:00Z" if resolved_at else None,
111	        options=[str(value).strip() for value in (row.get("options") or []) if str(value).strip()],
112	        question_set=TEST_QUESTION_SET_ID,
113	        question_set_label=TEST_QUESTION_SET_LABEL,
114	        classification=qtype or None,
115	        data_mode=_data_mode_for(qtype),
116	        target_date_text=resolved_at or None,
117	        closing_date_text=resolved_at or None,
118	        buckets=_question_buckets(row),
119	        data=metadata,
120	    )
121	
122	
123	def build_seeded_test_question_records() -> tuple[list[QuestionRecord], list[QuestionSetRecord]]:
124	    rows = _load_seed_rows()
125	    records = [_question_record_from_seed(row=row, seed_order=index) for index, row in enumerate(rows)]
126	    question_sets = [
127	        QuestionSetRecord(
128	            id=TEST_QUESTION_SET_ID,
129	            label=TEST_QUESTION_SET_LABEL,
130	            description=TEST_QUESTION_SET_DESCRIPTION,
131	        )
132	    ]
133	    return records, question_sets
134	
135	
136	def seed_test_question_fixture_db(tmp_path: Path) -> SeededTestQuestionFixture:
137	    graphs_dir = tmp_path / "graphs"
138	    graphs_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
139	    questions_path = graphs_dir / "questions.json"
140	    if questions_path.exists():
141	        questions_path.unlink()
142	
143	    storage_runtime = StorageRuntimeConfig(
144	        database_url=f"sqlite+pysqlite:///{(tmp_path / 'test_questions.db').as_posix()}",
145	        local_workspace_root=tmp_path / "workspace",
146	        project_root=tmp_path,
147	    )
148	    adapters = build_database_worker_storage_adapters(storage_runtime)
149	    questions, question_sets = build_seeded_test_question_records()
150	    adapters.graph_store_repository.save_questions(questions_path, questions, question_sets=question_sets)
151	    return SeededTestQuestionFixture(
152	        storage_runtime=storage_runtime,
153	        adapters=adapters,
154	        graphs_dir=graphs_dir,
155	        questions_path=questions_path,
156	    )
157	
158	
159	def load_seeded_test_question_rows(fixture: SeededTestQuestionFixture) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
160	    rows = fixture.adapters.question_catalog_repository.load_question_rows(fixture.questions_path)
161	    return sorted(rows, key=lambda row: int(((row.get("data") or {}).get("seed_order")) or 0))
162	
163	
164	def load_seeded_test_question_records(fixture: SeededTestQuestionFixture) -> list[QuestionRecord]:
165	    records = fixture.adapters.graph_store_repository.load_questions(fixture.questions_path)
166	    return sorted(records, key=lambda row: int(((row.data or {}).get("seed_order")) or 0))
167	
168	
169	def bootstrap_domain_pack_manifests_for_database(*, database_url: str) -> None:
170	    """Seed domain-pack config tables into the selected database when needed."""
171	    previous = os.environ.get("DATABASE_URL")
172	    try:
173	        os.environ["DATABASE_URL"] = database_url
174	        from services.worker.agents.ResearchDriver.domain_packs import (
175	            clear_domain_pack_manifest_cache,
176	            sync_domain_pack_manifests_to_database,
177	        )
178	
179	        clear_domain_pack_manifest_cache()
180	        sync_domain_pack_manifests_to_database(force=True)
181	        clear_domain_pack_manifest_cache()
182	    finally:
183	        if previous is None:
184	            os.environ.pop("DATABASE_URL", None)
185	        else:
186	            os.environ["DATABASE_URL"] = previous
187	
188	
189	__all__ = [
190	    "SeededTestQuestionFixture",
191	    "TEST_QUESTION_IDS",
192	    "TEST_QUESTION_SET_DESCRIPTION",
193	    "TEST_QUESTION_SET_ID",
194	    "TEST_QUESTION_SET_LABEL",
195	    "bootstrap_domain_pack_manifests_for_database",
196	    "build_seeded_test_question_records",
197	    "load_seeded_test_question_records",
198	    "load_seeded_test_question_rows",
199	    "seed_test_question_fixture_db",
200	]

448	def _upsert_question_records(
449	    session: Session,
450	    *,
451	    questions: list[QuestionRecord],
452	) -> None:
453	    for record in questions:
454	        candidate = _question_record_to_row(record)
455	        existing = session.get(QuestionRow, record.question_id)
456	        if existing is None:
457	            session.add(candidate)
458	            continue
459	        existing.title = candidate.title
460	        existing.type = candidate.type
461	        existing.targets = candidate.targets
462	        existing.source = candidate.source
463	        existing.domain = candidate.domain
464	        existing.question_set_id = candidate.question_set_id
465	        existing.keywords = candidate.keywords
466	        existing.metadata_ = candidate.metadata_
467	        existing.updated_at = utc_now()

470	def _graph_row_to_record(row: GraphRow, session: Session) -> GraphIndex:
471	    """Build a GraphIndex from the row, deriving membership from nodes.graph_id / edges.graph_id."""
472	    node_ids = list(session.scalars(select(NodeRow.node_id).where(NodeRow.graph_id == row.graph_id).order_by(NodeRow.node_id)).all())
473	    edge_ids = list(session.scalars(select(EdgeRow.edge_id).where(EdgeRow.graph_id == row.graph_id).order_by(EdgeRow.edge_id)).all())
474	    return GraphIndex(
475	        graph_id=row.graph_id,
476	        title=str(row.graph_id),
477	        node_ids=node_ids,
478	        edge_ids=edge_ids,
479	    )
480	
481	
482	def _node_row_to_record(row: NodeRow, session: Any = None) -> NodeRecord:
483	    node_type = row.type.value if hasattr(row.type, "value") else str(row.type)
484	    data: dict[str, Any] = {}
485	    if row.question_id:
486	        data["question_id"] = row.question_id
487	    # Load detail from type-specific table if session is available
488	    if session is not None:
489	        detail_data = _load_node_type_detail(session, row.node_id, row.type)
490	        if detail_data:
491	            data.update(detail_data)
492	    return NodeRecord(
493	        node_id=row.node_id,
494	        type=node_type,
495	        label=row.label,
496	        toi=None,
497	        baseline_p=row.baseline_p,
498	        posterior_p=row.posterior_p,
499	        instrument=None,
500	        split_from_driver_id=None,
501	        data=data,
502	        run_id=str(row.run_id or ""),
503	    )
504	
505	
506	def _load_node_type_detail(session: Any, node_id: str, node_type: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
507	    nt = node_type.value if hasattr(node_type, "value") else str(node_type)
508	    if nt == "target":
509	        row = session.get(TargetRow, node_id)
510	    elif nt == "driver":
511	        row = session.get(DriverRow, node_id)
512	    elif nt == "indicator":
513	        row = session.get(IndicatorRow, node_id)
514	    elif nt == "information":
515	        row = session.get(InformationNodeRow, node_id)
516	    else:
517	        return {}
518	    if row is None:
519	        return {}
520	    detail = dict(row.detail or {})
521	    if nt == "information" and row.file_location:
522	        nested_detail = dict(detail.get("detail") or {})
523	        nested_detail.setdefault("file_location", dict(row.file_location or {}))
524	        detail["detail"] = nested_detail
525	    return detail
526	
527	
528	def _edge_row_to_record(row: EdgeRow, session: Any = None) -> EdgeRecord:
529	    raw_edge_type = row.type.value if hasattr(row.type, "value") else str(row.type)
530	    edge_type = str(raw_edge_type or "").strip().lower()
531	    data = _load_edge_type_detail(session, row.edge_id, row.type) if session is not None else None
532	    weight = None
533	    if session is not None and edge_type == "conditional":
534	        cond_row = session.get(ConditionalEdgeRow, row.edge_id)
535	        if cond_row is not None:
536	            weight = cond_row.weight
537	    return EdgeRecord(
538	        edge_id=row.edge_id,
539	        from_node=row.from_node,
540	        to_node=row.to_node,
541	        toi=None,
542	        weight=weight,
543	        type=edge_type,
544	        data=data,
545	        run_id="",
546	    )
547	
548	
549	def _graph_record_to_row(record: GraphIndex, *, run_id: str = "") -> GraphRow:
550	    return GraphRow(
551	        graph_id=record.graph_id,
552	        run_id=run_id or record.graph_id,
553	        status=GraphStatus.ACTIVE,
554	        created_at=utc_now(),
555	        updated_at=utc_now(),
556	    )
557	
558

315	        "url": record.url,
316	        "label": record.label,
317	        "toi": record.toi,
318	        "target_date": record.target_date or record.toi,
319	        "options": list(record.options or []),
320	    }
321	    question_set = str(record.question_set or (record.data or {}).get("question_set") or "").strip()
322	    question_set_label = str(record.question_set_label or (record.data or {}).get("question_set_label") or "").strip()
323	    if question_set:
324	        row["question_set"] = question_set
325	    if question_set_label:
326	        row["question_set_label"] = question_set_label
327	    if record.classification:
328	        row["classification"] = record.classification
329	    if record.data_mode:
330	        row["data_mode"] = record.data_mode
331	    if record.unit:
332	        row["unit"] = record.unit
333	    if record.target_date_text:
334	        row["target_date_text"] = record.target_date_text
335	    if record.closing_date_text:
336	        row["closing_date_text"] = record.closing_date_text
337	    if record.buckets:
338	        row["buckets"] = list(record.buckets)
339	    if record.data:
340	        row["data"] = dict(record.data)
341	    return row

350	        "unit": record.unit,
351	        "data_mode": record.data_mode,
352	    }
353	    metadata_json = dict(record.data or {})
354	    metadata_json.update(
355	        {
356	            "url": record.url,
357	            "toi": record.toi,

359	            "target_date_text": record.target_date_text,
360	            "closing_date_text": record.closing_date_text,
361	        }
362	    )
363	    targets_json = _sanitize_json_payload(targets_json)

365	    keywords = _sanitize_json_payload((record.data or {}).get("keywords"))
366	    return QuestionRow(

def _question_row_to_record(row: QuestionRow) -> QuestionRecord:
    # Map new schema back to old QuestionRecord for backward compatibility.
    targets = dict(row.targets or {}) if row.targets else {}
    q_type = row.type.value if hasattr(row.type, "value") else str(row.type or "binary")
    type_to_classification = {
        "binary": "binary",
        "categorical": "categorical_outcome",
        "numeric_ordinal": "numeric_ordinal",
        "time_ordinal": "time_ordinal",
    }
    return QuestionRecord(
        question_id=row.question_id,
        url=str((row.metadata_ or {}).get("url", "")),
        label=row.title,
        toi=str((row.metadata_ or {}).get("toi", "")),
        target_date=str((row.metadata_ or {}).get("target_date", "")),
        options=list(targets.get("options", [])),
        question_set=row.question_set_id,
        question_set_label=None,
        classification=type_to_classification.get(q_type, "categorical_outcome"),
        data_mode=targets.get("data_mode"),
        unit=targets.get("unit"),
        target_date_text=str((row.metadata_ or {}).get("target_date_text", "")),
        closing_date_text=str((row.metadata_ or {}).get("closing_date_text", "")),
        buckets=targets.get("buckets"),
        data=dict(row.metadata_ or {}),
    )

def test_fs2_seeded_replay_features_come_from_db_rows_only(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    fixture = seed_test_question_fixture_db(tmp_path)
    rows = load_seeded_test_question_rows(fixture)

    assert not fixture.questions_path.exists()
    assert {str(row["classification"] or "").strip() for row in rows} >= {
        "binary",
        "categorical_outcome",
        "time_ordinal",
        "numeric_ordinal",
    }

    for row in rows:
        features = build_question_features(row)
        expected_fields = list(((row.get("data") or {}).get("expected_fs2_fields")) or [])
        for field in expected_fields:
            assert field in features, f"{row['id']}: missing DB-backed replay field {field!r}"

    Cross-FS: Map binary question type to categorical_outcome on SQL load (#316)
    
    A binary question created via POST /api/questions had its
    classification correctly set to 'categorical_outcome' on write
    (see services/api/mappers.py:282), but the SQL load path's
    _question_row_to_record was re-deriving classification from the
    type column with a stale map that returned 'binary' for binary
    questions. Downstream, core.question_completeness rejects
    'binary' as not in CLASSIFICATIONS and fails run-research with
    "missing classification".
    
    Stale map predates the canonical-classifications normalization
    (research/question_classification.py:CLASSIFICATIONS); it never
    caught up. The baselines and dispatch code already treats
    'binary' and 'categorical_outcome' as equivalent, so widening
    the load mapping to canonical 'categorical_outcome' aligns
---

commit 60456851107973c32a540c3c2d3ce73325baa88f
Author: Sumeet Saini <57606824+kungfusaini@users.noreply.github.com>
Date:   Thu May 21 08:21:28 2026 +0100

    Cross-FS: Map binary question type to categorical_outcome on SQL load (#316)
    
    A binary question created via POST /api/questions had its
    classification correctly set to 'categorical_outcome' on write
    (see services/api/mappers.py:282), but the SQL load path's
    _question_row_to_record was re-deriving classification from the
    type column with a stale map that returned 'binary' for binary
    questions. Downstream, core.question_completeness rejects
    'binary' as not in CLASSIFICATIONS and fails run-research with
    "missing classification".
    
    Stale map predates the canonical-classifications normalization
    (research/question_classification.py:CLASSIFICATIONS); it never
    caught up. The baselines and dispatch code already treats
    'binary' and 'categorical_outcome' as equivalent, so widening
    the load mapping to canonical 'categorical_outcome' aligns
    storage with the rest of the codebase.
    
    Failure exemplar from staging:
    
      job_id:        585b01baf1404b899900f3357d415023
      error_code:    worker_execution_failed
      error_message: QuestionCompletenessError: ...
                     demo_set_q_001 (binary): missing classification
    
    Adds a round-trip regression test in tests/test_question_completeness.py
    that exercises the full create -> save_graph_store_bundle ->
    load_questions -> validate_questions_for_pipeline path and would
    have caught this when SPI-236 (or the original load path) was
    written.
    
    Co-authored-by: Claude Opus 4.7 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>

@@ -63,3 +63,71 @@ def test_validate_questions_for_pipeline_accepts_complete_categorical_outcome()
             }
         ]
     )

+
+def test_binary_question_round_trips_to_categorical_outcome_classification(tmp_path) -> None:
+    """A binary question created via the API mapper, persisted, and reloaded
+    via the SQL repository must come back with classification='categorical_outcome'
+    — the canonical form the completeness validator accepts. Regression for the
+    GUI 'Run Research' failure where binary questions round-tripped as
+    classification='binary' and got rejected with 'missing classification'."""
+    from datetime import date
+
+    from packages.storage import (
+        GraphIndex,
+        StorageRuntimeConfig,
+        build_database_worker_storage_adapters,
+        resolve_graph_store_paths,
+    )
+    from services.api.mappers import normalize_question_create
+
+    storage_runtime = StorageRuntimeConfig(
+        database_url=f"sqlite+pysqlite:///{(tmp_path / 'binary_round_trip.db').as_posix()}",
+        local_workspace_root=tmp_path / "workspace",
+        project_root=tmp_path,
+    )
+    graphs_dir = tmp_path / "graphs"
+    graphs_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    adapters = build_database_worker_storage_adapters(storage_runtime)
+    paths = resolve_graph_store_paths(graphs_dir=graphs_dir, questions_path=graphs_dir / "questions.json")
+
+    question = normalize_question_create(
+        {
+            "label": "Will UK CPI inflation be above 3% in December 2026?",
+            "question_type": "binary",
+            "closing_date": date(2026, 12, 31),
+            "question_set_id": "demo_set",
+            "question_set_label": "Demo Questions",

590	        self,
591	        *,
592	        driver_title: str,
593	        driver_query: str,
594	        source_titles: Sequence[str],
595	        max_indicators: int,

599	        _, user_prompt = prepare_extraction_prompt(
600	            driver_title=driver_title,

602	            source_titles=source_titles,
603	            max_indicators=max_indicators,

608	    def _build_full_message_list(self, **kwargs: Any) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
609	        messages: list[dict[str, Any]] = [
610	            {
611	                "role": "system",
612	                "content": self.system_prompt,
613	                **({} if os.environ.get("LITELLM_PROXY_BASE_URL") else {"cache_control": {"type": "ephemeral"}}),
614	            }
615	        ]
616	        messages.extend(self.build_messages(**kwargs))
617	        return messages
618	
619	    def get_response_model(self, **_: Any) -> type[Any] | None:
620	        return self.response_model
621	
622	    def postprocess(self, result: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
623	        if hasattr(result, "model_dump"):
624	            data = result.model_dump(mode="json")
625	        elif isinstance(result, dict):
626	            data = dict(result)
627	        else:
628	            data = {}
629	        driver_title = clean_text(kwargs.get("driver_title"))
630	        return normalize_indicator_rows(
631	            rows_in=data.get("indicators") or [],
632	            driver_title=driver_title,
633	        )
634	
635	    def run(self, **kwargs: Any) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
636	        """Single-call path routed through the shared proxy-safe LLM runtime."""
637	        parsed = self._runtime.complete_structured(
638	            messages=self._build_full_message_list(**kwargs),
639	            response_model=self.response_model,
640	        )
641	        return self.postprocess(parsed, **kwargs)

649	def extract_indicator_candidates_for_driver_payload(
650	    *,
651	    driver_title: str,
652	    driver_query: str,
653	    source_titles: Sequence[str],
654	    max_indicators: int = 4,
655	    llm_enabled: bool = True,
656	    fallback_pack_name: str = "generic_arxiv",
657	    precomputed_llm_result: Any = None,
658	    indicator_extraction_model: str | None = None,
659	    indicator_extraction_free_model: str | None = None,
660	) -> dict[str, Any]:
661	    pack = build_retrieval_pack_for_name(fallback_pack_name)
662	    max_indicators_effective = effective_indicator_limit(max_indicators)
663	    cleaned_source_titles: list[str] = []
664	    for raw in source_titles:
665	        title = clean_text(raw, max_len=240)
666	        if title and title not in cleaned_source_titles:
667	            cleaned_source_titles.append(title)
668	    has_supporting_sources = bool(cleaned_source_titles)
669	    task_id = "FS3.indicator_extraction" if has_supporting_sources else "FS3.indicator_extraction_free"
670	    model_override = indicator_extraction_model if has_supporting_sources else indicator_extraction_free_model
671	
672	    llm_error = ""
673	    llm_quality_failure: dict[str, Any] | None = None
674	    if llm_enabled:
675	        try:
676	            if precomputed_llm_result is not None:
677	                rows = precomputed_llm_result
678	            else:
679	                tool = DriverIndicatorExtractionTool(task_id=task_id, model=model_override)

497	def _indicator_dedupe_lane_bucket(row: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
498	    sm = str(row.get("source_mode") or "").strip().lower()
499	    if sm == "llm_best_effort_no_sources":
500	        return "synthetic_llm"
501	    if "pack" in sm or "fallback" in sm:
502	        return "pack_fallback"
503	    return "evidence_llm"
504	
505	
506	def _indicator_name_key(name: str) -> str:
507	    return clean_text(name, max_len=120).lower()

876	def batch_extract_indicator_candidates(
877	    drivers: Sequence[dict[str, Any]],
878	    *,
879	    max_indicators: int = 4,
880	    llm_enabled: bool = True,
881	    fallback_pack_name: str = "generic_arxiv",
882	    indicator_extraction_model: str | None = None,
883	    indicator_extraction_free_model: str | None = None,
884	) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
885	    """Batch-extract indicator candidates for multiple drivers.
886	
887	    Uses the Anthropic Batch API when available, falling back to sequential.
888	    Each entry in ``drivers`` should have keys: driver_title, driver_query, source_titles.
889	    Returns a list of extraction payloads in the same order.
890	    """

> AGENT

The gitleaks hook is flagging a known dev key (`sk-litellm-dev-key`) in archived agent_rules YAML files that came in from `origin/dev`. They're already on `dev` (commit `82ee89e`, PR #271), and the same files don't trip the hook when committing on dev itself — the hook only runs on staged-for-this-commit content. Since the merge stages those dev files, the hook trips.

The dev key is a literal local-only LiteLLM placeholder, not a real secret, but I want to confirm before adding a `.gitleaksignore` entry as part of this merge commit (which would be slightly out of scope for the PR).

> AGENT

PR #293 is now `MERGEABLE` ([`11cb06a`](https://github.com/elijahintelligence/elijah/pull/293)). The new conflicts came from PR #290 landing on dev — all four were the same `generic_arxiv` → `generic_web` change colliding with this PR's `indicator_extraction_model` / `indicator_extraction_free_model` additions; resolved by keeping dev's new default and PR 293's new fields together in:

- `core/research_driver/indicator_graph_apply.py` (config default + call site)
- `core/research_driver/indicator_extraction.py` (`batch_extract_indicator_candidates` signature)
- `core/pipelines/research_driver_components.py` (config construction)
- `services/worker/research_driver_question_pipeline.py` (apply call)
